# Project status and critical path

Maintained by the project-manager session (-c5). Updated 2026-10-19, 19:45, after status replies from the
hooking session (-6d, flycrane-meta) and the transport / ES session (-0f, flycrane-evo). Companion documents:
`code_quality_control.md` (audit, all defects fixed), `evaluation_protocol.md` (what counts as a result),
`insight_tasks_2026-10-19.md` (identifiability probes, regression checks).

## 1. State in one paragraph

The plant and the observation contract are settled and verified: construction / sequence env of flycrane-meta,
3 flyhook drones, 51-dim per-drone frame x 3 history, 5 actions; regression checks pass on izar at 5166973
(reset 3.9e-5 m, pin 1.05 N, hold risk <= 0.27). Of the three things the goal needs, **hooking** is training on
that plant (legacy arms + the fresh three-drone run all_s1, vbox_mu 0.6 / pin_kp 150 / 40 s episodes) and has
not yet produced a 3-drone joint hold from free starts; the D10 / D15 parity ladder (2026-10-20/21) showed our
PPO trainer, not the plant or the reward, is why single-drone catching is late (rsl_rl on the same plant
catches at v84's pace, R0 391 catches at 19k; no flag or code arm of our trainer does), so hooking moved to
rsl_rl; the fine-tune line from R0 collapsed in every arm (a converged actor with an uncalibrated critic) and
was closed on the user's direction (retrain from scratch, D16s); the from-scratch three-drone arms reach the bar
but never learn the swing-out (swung_out gate failed by 98-100 % of dwells vs R0 70 %), and the single-drone
discriminator D16s1 (2026-10-24) lagged R0 the same way, and the single-drone ablation ladders (2026-10-24/26)
named the blockers: the sector penalty (an exploration barrier, the one hard blocker), box-size DR (delays the
onset), hold_start_end 0.15 (weak) and the episode structure (the last single-drone lever, not adoptable under
D18; line paused). The three-drone arm d16f (sector off, R0 geometry, hold start 0.0; 3229542) is the FIRST to
learn the swing-out and catches at 100-146 per tracker row from 24k, reaching two holders in 12-18 % of
episodes, but it CANNOT reach the joint hold: two holders pull 2-3x the virtual box's slide budget (adjacent and
opposite pairs alike), and the vbox termination from 41.7k removes the all-three states instead of teaching a
lighter pull. The user's D19 'pull gently' (per-drone force penalty, safe force 0.3 W, light joint hold) runs as
d16p 3230954 with d16f as its control; its catch onset is in (16k: 80 vs d16f's 14) and but at 18k the 12k-16k pull gap is gone (single-holder pull q50 8.8 N in both runs) and its two-holder risk is as bad as d16f's (q50 3.0, 0 spells with median risk < 1 in any row of either run); the force penalty at 0.5 per N is ~13 % of a holder's hold income and the vbox penalty is flat at the cap, so the policy drifts back to pulling hard; no pair-type selection in either run. 22k repeats it (pooled 16k-22k: 0 safe two-holder spells of 39); the user's D20 launched d16q 3231580 (d16p + hold_force_penalty 2.5) with d16p as its 0.5 per N comparison, and d16q (onset 12k) is ahead of d16p at matched rows through 18k on catches (177 vs 64), safe >= 1 s two-holder spells (1 / 6 / 6 on three rows; d16p none before 30k) and all-three steps, with pull lower but drifting up (4.4 -> 5.7 N) and only 1.5 % of its all-three steps safe because the three drones together lift the whole box weight (sum F_up / m g q50 1.04), leaving no friction budget: the all-three wall is vertical unloading, not horizontal pull and not the train/eval friction gap, and it is universal (d16f 0.88 m g too), unevenly shared (the top drone carries ~55 %), and untouched by the per-drone force penalty, which cut horizontal pull only; the team's upward pull is the candidate next lever D22, but d16q is lowering it unprompted (median 1.04 -> 0.65 m g by 22k, all-three safe share 1.5 -> 22 %, longest protocol run 0.54 s of the 1.0 s needed, at the cost of one slack wire 10 % of the time), so D22 waits for 24k-28k; §4 D17); and the D24 probe of a pull input uncovered a PLANT DEFECT (D25, 2026-10-29 ~18:55): the simulated drone weighs 1.62 kg (a phantom 1.0 kg URDF link) against the controller's and the real Falcon's 0.6017 kg, thrust-to-weight 1.33 in sim vs ~2.8 real, so every hooking checkpoint so far is on the wrong drone and the user decides whether to fix now (relaunch d16t as d16u) or after d16t; **transport on that plant does not
exist** (the meta transport family is the evo hover_rand plant re-observed, and the evo ES track runs a 45-dim
drone-only actor that cannot see box heading and whose plastic head has not beaten its warm start); the

protocol's static controls, and no plastic-vs-frozen comparison on any plant. The critical path is therefore:
hooking joint hold -> a 51x3 transport teacher on the construction plant -> the static controls -> the plastic
head and the switching test in the sequence env.

Rule: compare generations only within a run, quote eval.py numbers for absolute claims. The 4-scenario draw moves the level of a generation, not the ranking: the ES ranks antithetic pair differences on the same scenarios (paired eval), so the draw cancels. The selection noise that does compete with the plasticity effect is chaotic episode-to-episode variation of a fixed genome on a fixed scenario (pair_diff_std 55-80 vs a sigma-0.01 signal of a few points, SNR ~0.02 per genome before pairing, ~5x less noise variance with pairing); that plus the fitness composition (heading weight, relaxed success threshold) is the plausible cause of the v14-v17 plateau. ## 3. Critical path (ordered; each step gates the next)

1. **Joint hold from free starts** (A). Gate: protocol joint hold v1.1 (all attached, wires taut, vbox risk < 1, 100 steps)
   in >= 10 % of free-start episodes at final curriculum settings, deterministic, 3 evaluation seeds.
   Sub-gate found 2026-10-20: the single-drone catch on the construction plant (0.30-0.45 per episode at the final rule)
   is far below the v84r2 release recipe (0.91), so the joint hold is limited by catch skill before team effects;
   D10 parity test (V- / T-ladder, eval only) showed the cause is the training side, not the env (v84r2 0.87 vs ours 0.28 in the same physics); the gate is measured WITH collision termination
   from the next tracker edit on (D9), and the adaptive catch curriculum gets hysteresis at the next restart (D8 v).
**Proposed revision (PM, 2026-10-20, pending the user's D10 stage-2 decision):** replace 'joint hold by from-scratch RL' with a teacher route mirroring D1a: (1a) distil v84r2 into a 51x3 base-ref student on the construction single plant (runbook under D10, gate catch >= 0.80 / 1 s hold >= 0.75); (1b) RL fine-tune that student with the meta trainer on the three-drone plant via --init_checkpoint for the team effects (hold duration, collision avoidance at the D9 radius), with the curriculum starting at the final rule (no catch curriculum needed, which also removes the D8 cycling) and the five-term reward; gate unchanged (protocol joint hold >= 10 %, measured WITH collision termination). The running legacy / scratch arms continue as the baseline until (1b) is measured. 3224576 (v84r2 x3 zero-shot in the three-drone plant) tells how far (1b) has to go.

(1b) config / cost / risk (-6d, 2026-10-20; every setting is an existing flag, no code): launch_meta with seq_hook1 + seq_hook (512 + 128 tasks; the single plant in the mix is the cheapest catch-retention guard), --history_len 3 (153 = 51x3), --init_checkpoint <student model.pt> (actor only; fresh critic with --critic_warmup 100-200 before any policy update), freeze_obs_norm=true (keep the student's normaliser; a drifting normaliser under a new state distribution was the -400 sigma breaker), reward_mode=simple + rs_k_floor 40 + the v84fresh_s1 env cfg (vbox_mu 0.6, pin_kp 150, wide box DR, bar_band_random, obs_ref=base, 40 s, joint_hold_steps 100, end_on_hold_success=false), final rule from step 0 (adaptive_curriculum=false, curriculum_steps=1: radius / chord / hold milestone at final, v84 aids off, no D8 cycling), collision_curriculum_steps=1 (NOT 0: with 0 the soft close penalty is never built, rd['close'] exists only for > 0; with 1 cfr = 1 after one step, termination on and close penalty at close_penalty_end from the start) with collision_dist = the D9 value, hold_start_frac as in the current seq_hook1, vbox_terminate_from=-1 as in the simple arms. Compute: ~5.6 s / it (~640 it / h) on one V100; if v84r2 x3 already holds zero-shot (3224576), a measurable hold at the first tracker point (<= 1k it, ~1.5 h) and the 10 % gate plausibly within 2-5k it (3-8 h); if it gets third attachments but no holds, +5-10k it (8-16 h). Tracker as now plus the with-collision row (the pending job edit). Main risk: catch erosion as in v84simple_ft (54 -> 0 catches in 3k it from a fresh-critic start). Guards, cheapest first, all flags: (1) critic_warmup 200 + freeze_obs_norm; (2) smaller steps: desired_kl 0.005 or fixed meta_lr 1e-4, entropy_coef ~0, max_std ~0.4; (3) the single plant in the mix; (4) operating rule: tracker every 500 it, stop / roll back if single-plant reach1 < 0.7 or per-dwell catch < 6 %; (5) only if 1-4 fail: a BC anchor to the teacher (lambda ||mu - mu_teacher||^2 on teacher-labelled states, ~40 lines + the remap). Layer freezing has no flag.

Schedule check for (1b) (-6d, read-only on ceaa335): with adaptive_curriculum=false and curriculum_steps=1 every progress-driven quantity (radius, chord, hold milestone, spawn distance, swing / hook assist) is 1.0 from control step 1; only the very first reset at ctrl_step 0 gets the close spawns and assists, once. Separate schedules: joint_hold_ramp inactive (requirement fixed at 100 steps); hold_start decays 0.5 -> 0 over 65k steps on seq_hook1 (restarts from 0.5 in a fresh run); the vbox ramp / vbox_terminate_from are legacy-reward only, under reward_mode=simple the box enters only through rs_hold·taut·g(risk) at full strength from step 0 but only on attached drones, so a drone faces the box weight exactly when it holds. --init_checkpoint loads the policy only; ctrl_step and critic_warmup start at 0 (the resumed legacy arm instead restored env_ctrl_step, which is why its collision onset hit mid-run).

2. **Transport teacher on the construction plant, 51x3** (B). Gate: protocol transport success (0.10 m /
   0.35 rad held 50 steps, all attached) >= 80 % from attached starts on the train split of mass. This is the
   step with no owner and no code today; it is the longest pole after A.
3. **Static controls** (D) on the same task and splits. Gate: H1 (oracle beats static on the test split). If
   H1 fails, widen the task distribution before any plastic work.
4. **Plastic head within-task** (E). Gate: H2 and H3 on table A.
5. **Chain + switching** (F) in the sequence env. Gate: H4 on table B; table C with the seed spread.

Steps 3-5 reuse the imitation + ANIL + switching-head code that already exists in flycrane-meta; the new work
is the data (step 2) and the controls (step 3), not the algorithms.

## 4. Decisions needed from the user

- **D1 is now two halves (2026-10-20).** **D1a, 51x3 transport student on the hover_rand plant (half a day, -0f's estimate):** the paper-obs teacher's 45-dim frame is slot-for-slot a subset of the 51 frame except the drone-ID one-hot (replaced by the assigned-wall centre) and the INDI residual (new). Option A (recommended by -0f): DAgger distillation in flycrane_meta/envs/transport.py, student 51x3 -> 5 actions (hook actor shape), labels = teacher mean action from env.paper_frames(), MSE, 4096 envs: ~1 h coding + 2-4 h V100 + 15 min protocol eval; expected ~90 % protocol success. Option B: MAPPO retrain on 51x3 with the warm-start recipe, ~14-18 h V100. Pre-checks DONE by -0f (read-only, 2026-10-20): (a) action scaling: evo teacher and meta transport env consume the same 5-dim raw setpoint action unchanged (transport.py inherits MARLHoverRandEnv, no action transform; the IndiController only adds the residual observation); the teacher outputs 2 tanh in [-2, 2], MetaPolicy's mean is an unbounded linear head, so teacher mean actions are valid labels in native units, no inverse map. (b) 51x3 is a flag, not code: configure_env_cfg sets actor_history_len (transport.py default 3), collect_demos.py stores the full stack, train_imitation.py --history_len 3. (c) The pipeline exists (scripts/collect_demos.py with DART noise + DAgger via --student, scripts/train_imitation.py); the ONE missing piece is a teacher adapter in models.load_policy for the evo train_mappo.py checkpoint reading env.unwrapped.paper_frames() (45-dim incl. the drone-ID one-hot), i.e. flycrane_evo PPOActorPolicy wrapped to the per-drone lane layout, ~30-40 lines. Half-day estimate stands. Needs the user's go to -0f. Label-integrity checks (-0f, read-only): the teacher's drone-ID one-hot is eye(3) indexed by the drone's position in cfg.possible_agents and transport.py builds lane i from the same list, so meta lane i == teacher one-hot i; on hover_rand the box-pose 12, goal-error 3 and difference-matrix 9 slots are the very tensors paper_frames() feeds the teacher (payload COM in env-local world axes, world rotation, goal_pos_error, R_goal R_box^T), so no remap and no corruption risk. **D1a runbook (-0f, 2026-10-20; nothing executed; one word from the user starts it):** (1) adapter EvoTeacher (~40 lines, flycrane_meta/models.py or evo_teacher.py) wrapping flycrane_evo PPOActorPolicy on teacher_paperobs_best_1018.pt, .mean(obs51) with teacher H = 1 and student H = 3 decoupled; remap x45 = [box pose 0:12 | eye(3)[lane] | own state 15:33 | goal err + diff 33:45] (drops wall centre 12:15 and INDI 45:51); slot orders verified against the builders (transport.py _get_observations and hover_env's obs_set='paper' branch / paper_frames), rotation slots row-major on both sides from the same (E, 3, 3) tensors, so the remap is a pure slice-and-insert, no transpose; load_policy branch on 'flat_weights'; new --history_len flag in collect_demos so the stored stack is 3 frames. (2) collect on izar, 1 V100: round 0 teacher drives (collect_demos.py --family transport --history_len 3 --num_tasks 128 --envs_per_task 16 --steps 3000 --every 2 --warmup 200 --noisy_frac 0.5 --noise_std 0.3, ~25 min), DAgger rounds 1-2 student drives / teacher labels (--student logs/imit/d1a_r<k-1>/model.pt), matching the existing dagger1/2 recipe. (3) train_imitation.py --variant multitask (plain BC, single family) --history_len 3 --hidden 512 512 --steps 30000 --action_norm false (labels are native [-2, 2] setpoints), ~20 min per round, MetaPolicy checkpoint. (4) new scripts/eval_protocol.py (~30 lines: meta transport env, history 3, 1024 envs, seed 123, flycrane_evo evaluate() with a MetaPolicy wrapper), prints the eval.py line. **Pass: protocol success >= 80 % AND survival within 5 points of the teacher's 87-90 %** (the student must not add crash modes). (5) one izar job, qos normal, 6 h, 1 V100: r0 collect -> train -> r1 -> r2 -> protocol eval of r0 / r1 / r2; code in a fresh copy ~/projects/meta_d1a (meta + hook + evo at dev state INCLUDING the uncommitted evo eval helpers, so **the evo commit comes first**, or the tree is copied as is); ~/projects/split untouched; 20 min smoke on the debug queue first (new flags); hook-side regression_checks not needed (no hook code path runs). Total ~1 h coding + 20 min smoke + ~4 h job, result the same day. **D1b, transport teacher on the construction plant (sequence.py, box with bars, attached starts, mass / CoM / size DR):** no owner, no estimate yet; the merge waits on it. -0f's read (2026-10-20, from construction.py / sequence.py): the hover_rand student would meet (a) different attachment physics: three flyhook articulations (drone + 8-link flexible wire + 0.30 kg hook) held by the hook-grasp spring-damper instead of flycrane's rigid-rope joints, so cable direction, force transmission and the slack-wire behaviour differ; this is the one expected to break a hover_rand policy outright; (b) the sequence task always starts in the hook phase (box on pedestal, drones unattached), so the transport policy is only valid after all three hooks attach: a sequencing issue, which IS the merge; (c) box half-extents (0.275, 0.275, 0.16) with bars, mass 0.9-1.9 kg, optional CoM offset and size DR: mild. The teacher's 45-dim frame is fully computable from the 51-dim construction frame (box pose 12, own state 18, target error 12 + a drone-ID one-hot from the lane index), so the SAME teacher can label on the construction plant without env support, but only while its behaviour is valid there, which is unknown because of (a). **D1b probe (not launched, not asked):** run the teacher through the 51 -> 45 remap on sequence.py with hooks pre-attached and read survival + protocol transport success: ~1 h code (remap wrapper + pre-attached reset) + 1 h debug job. If it holds ~80 % or more, DAgger fine-tuning of the D1a student on that plant with the same teacher suffices; if it collapses, a MAPPO retrain on the construction plant (paper-obs actor + privileged critic, ~1 day). Remap caveat for that probe: construction.py's box-pose slot is the block BASE point (block_pos + R_box ref, 16 cm below the centre, the hook task's basket origin) and its goal error is target_base - base, while the teacher expects the payload COM and the goal error to the COM, so the remap must shift back by R_box ref (and R_target ref for the goal), ref being the cfg constant; all other slots match. A CoM-offset DR on that plant (D4) moves the true COM off the block origin in a way neither frame reports, which is exactly the hidden variable the plastic head is meant to absorb. Teacher crash types on hover_rand (the ~10 % residual) are unknown without a 15 min probe (eval.py logs only time-out vs terminated); -0f's guess: cable angle at the basket and drone-drone proximity (< 0.5 m). Original D1 text: Who builds the 51x3 transport teacher on the construction plant, and when. Options: (a) the evo session
  after v17 ends (45 h), using its MAPPO paper recipe; (b) start now on ALC or the free izar slot, in parallel
  with v17; (c) the hooking session after A reaches its gate. My recommendation: (b), owner -0f, since the
  recipe and the person-hours are there and v17 needs only a watcher. The ES / 45-dim track then ends with its
  plastic-on / plastic-off eval and a note.
- **D2. Seeds.** Protocol asks 3 training seeds; a hooking run is ~48 h on one GPU. Proposal: 1 training seed per
  skill until gates 1-2 pass, then 3 seeds for tables A-C only.
- **D3. The between-task claim.** The probes show the switch itself is frame-visible. Proposal: the claim becomes
  "the plastic head keeps the correct load compensation across the switch" (the hidden load each drone carries
  changes at the catch and at lift-off), tested as method 7 vs static. Needs your confirmation before E / F
  are designed.
- **D5. Hold-pose reward for attached drones while others catch.** The v84 hold pose leans out + up, so two hooked drones slide the box. Options: (a) penalise the horizontal component of an attached drone's pull / reward wire verticality while not all are attached; (b) a hold bonus while others catch, with the same verticality condition; (c) wait for the vbox penalty to reach full weight (~it 37.5k, ~10 h) and decide at it 38k. Recommendation: (c) now, (a) prepared in code for the next restart. Done: flycrane-meta f21ac6c adds `w_hold_horizontal` (default 0 = off) and `hold_horizontal_free` (1.0 N): per attached drone, while not all are attached, -w x dt x max(0, |horizontal pin reaction| - free). Suggested starting weight 2.0 (a 5 N lean costs ~8 per second per drone, comparable to the vbox penalty at full weight near risk 1.6). Not synced to izar; switch on only at a restart.
- **D5 / D6 superseded (2026-10-19 late).** Per-term eval (3223932, single plant, it 30k vs 32k, mean return per episode): vbox -0.26 vs -0.22 (negligible), catch 7.1 -> 3.9, hold_success 27.4 -> 15.7, hook_proximity 13.9 -> 15.2, hook_swing 4.8 -> 7.1, hook_above_box -50.2 vs -48.8 (dominates everything). So the stricter vbox is not why the 32k policy avoids the bar; catch and hold both shrank while proximity / swing shaping grew (shaping farmed), and the shared-policy incentive from the 40 s 3-drone episodes is the remaining suspect. User decision (with -6d): simplify the reward to five terms, `reward_mode='simple'` (flycrane-meta b482fea, local): reach = potential shaping on hook-to-bar distance + missing chord angle; catch +C / drop -C; hold = H x taut x g(risk), g = 1 up to 0.8 falling to -1 at 1.2; success = protocol v1.1 for 1 and 3 drones; safety = fail, close, over-box depth, action rate. Legacy reward kept verbatim as `_rewards_legacy`; trainer `--fresh_critic`. Two runs in parallel: fine-tune from the it 31k actor with a fresh critic, and from scratch. Gates passed (legacy identical to 3 decimals per term on it 30k; regression_checks pass on 8b03204). Reach is telescoping (rs_gamma 1.0; at 0.99 it paid +775 / episode for staying far), Phi = -15 |hook - bar| - 10 max(0, chord_required - chord). Over-box rs_k_box 20 (~ -7 / episode vs catch 20, success 145). Runs (seed 1, cfg = v84fresh_s1 + reward_mode simple, joint_hold_steps 100, end_on_hold_success false, vbox_terminate_from -1, rs_k_box 20): **v84simple_ft** (from it 31k actor / optimizer / curriculum, fresh critic with 100 warm-up its; izar 3223963 + 3223964, tracker 3223965, labels sft_) and **v84simple_s1** (scratch; 3223966 + 3223967 nice 100, tracker 3223968, labels ss1_). Legacy v84fresh_s1 (3223831 / 3223832 / tracker 3223927) kept as baseline pending the user's call. Commits b482fea, 8b03204, 09aacff reviewed and pushed. First tracker points (2026-10-19 late): sft_32000 single 20/136 catches (31k ref 65/165), protocol joint hold 0.118, critic warm-up dip as expected; 3-drone 54 catches, 2-attached 0.6 % (ref 7.9 %), risk > 1 at 2 attached 0.88; fly_low 22 / blowup 4 terminations (legacy 1-3); hold term +69 / episode vs catch +6 (watch: hold must not outpay further catches). Legacy f3_34000 recovering under the vbox ramp: single 66/163, protocol 0.337; 3-drone 121 catches / 128 eps, 2-attached 10.6 %, risk > 1 at 2 attached down to 0.196, protocol joint hold still 0. Legacy is currently the strongest arm; its outward pull at 2 attached (risk > 1 subset, now 20 % of those steps) fell from 4.98 N at 30k to 2.93 N at 34k: it is learning to pull less outward. fly_low diagnostic (263cb32, tests only) in all trackers from their next point. Scratch ss1_1000: 1 catch, reach +4.6 / episode. sft_33000: 3-drone worse (catches 54 -> 39, 2-attached 0.6 -> 0.4 %, 2nd attach reached 2.3 -> 0.7 %, hold +69 -> +46, over_box -7.8 -> -12.5; legacy ref 10.6 % / 17 %); single slightly up (23 catches, protocol 0.132). fly_low explained: unattached drones (n_attached 0) sink to z 0.15-0.19 m with the hook ~1.1 m below the box base, not chasing a low bar; the simple reward dropped the legacy dense alive term (+15.6 / episode) and the -40 one-off termination barely propagates at gamma 0.99. Proposed safety floor term -k dt (z_floor - z)+, z_floor = min_drone_height + 0.3 (decision D7 after sft_34000).
- **D6 (historical). Catch avoidance under the stricter virtual-box check.** After the it 31k restart (mu 0.8 -> 0.6, ramp ~8) the single-drone policy flies 9 cm higher and halves its time near the bar in 1000 iterations; the pin is ruled out by A/B. The vbox penalty can be escaped by not catching, which is cheaper than learning a vertical hold. Options: (a) wait for 34k (~3 h) and see whether the curriculum easing restores catching; (b) restart from it 31k with D5 option (b), a hold bonus for attached drones conditioned on a near-vertical wire, so holding correctly pays more than avoiding; (c) restart from 31k with mu 0.8 on the single plant only. Recommendation: (a) for the trend, then (b) if catching has not recovered, since (b) rewards the behaviour the chain needs and (c) only hides the slide problem. Done: flycrane-meta 9aef9f1 adds `w_hold_vertical` (default 0 = off) and `hold_vertical_cos0` (0.9, ~26 deg): per attached drone with a taut wire, while not all are attached, +w x dt x clamp((cos(wire to vertical) - cos0) / (1 - cos0), 0, 1). izar synced to 9aef9f1 with both optional terms at 0 (behaviour-neutral). Per-term returns eval running (3223932: it 30k vs 32k, training reward cfg, vbox weight fixed at 8.2; 34k to follow).
- **D7 (APPROVED by the user in session -6d, 2026-10-19; executed 2026-10-20).** (i) floor term `rs_k_floor` 40 / `rs_floor_z` 0.45 m (f701abb) added to reward_mode simple; scratch arm restarted as v84simple_s1f. (ii) fine-tune arm v84simple_ft stopped after collapsing to 0 catches at sft_35000; GPU moved to the legacy seed replicate v84fresh_s2. Chronology and numbers in section 7.

The vbox penalty can be escaped by not catching, which is cheaper than learning a vertical hold. Recommendation: (a) for the trend, then (b) if catching has not recovered, since (b) rewards the behaviour the chain needs and (c) only hides the slide problem. - **D7 (APPROVED by the user in session -6d, 2026-10-19 late).** (i) in progress: check job 3224388 (regression_checks + 3-drone diag on sft_33000 with rs_k_floor 0 vs 40), then sync f701abb to the live tree (off by default), cancel scratch v84simple_s1 keeping checkpoints, launch v84simple_s1f with rs_k_floor 40 (tracker labels ssf_). DONE 2026-10-20: 3224388 PASS (regression checks unchanged; floor 0 vs 40 on sft_33000 identical in every term except `floor` -0.86 / episode). Live tree synced (off). v84simple_s1 cancelled (checkpoints to ~2k kept); **v84simple_s1f** launched = same cfg + rs_k_floor 40 both families, seed 1: train 3224394, follow-up 3224395, tracker 3224396, labels ssf_, nice 100 (pending on priority). NOTES entry 1849734. ssf_2000 vs ss1_2000 (k 40 vs 0, scratch, single seed, noisy): 3-drone catches 0 vs 19, fly_low 7 vs 29, floor return only -0.1 / episode (drones rarely below 0.45 m); no action, next look ssf_4000. (ii) EXECUTED 2026-10-20 (no 34k tracker point; decided on sft_35000): fine-tune collapsed to 0 catches on both plants at 35k (single 20 -> 23 -> 0 of ~130; 3-drone 54 -> 39 -> 0); v84simple_ft stopped (3223963 / 3223964 / 3223965). **v84fresh_s2** launched = izar_v84freshd.sbatch with seed 2 only: train 3224488, follow-up 3224489, tracker 3224490, labels fs2_. NOTES dfd58aa. Legacy f3_36000 dipped (single 41/154, protocol 0.24; 3-drone 76 catches, 2-attached 4.2 %, 2nd attach 9.4 %) while safety kept improving (risk > 1 at 2 attached 0.034, outward pull q50 2.86 N over ALL 2-attached steps vs 5.0 N at 30k: real, not a subset artefact). f3_38000 = best legacy point, no D8: 3-drone catches 55 -> 121 -> 76 -> 164 (32k..38k, 128 eps), 1st / 2nd / 3rd attach reached 83 / 34 / 2.3 %, **first protocol joint hold 0.008 (1 / 128)**, risk > 1 at 2 attached 0.023 (median 0.45); per-episode vbox flat at -2.5 while catch 8.6 -> 25.3 and hold_success 34 -> 92 (the ramp, now at full weight, is not suppressing catching; 36k was a transient). Single: 73 / ~150 catches, protocol 0.383. Curriculum at final (progress 1.00, radius 0.050 m); training catch rate 0.25. Bottleneck: the 3rd attach (2.3 %); most 3-drone episodes end with two attached. Third-catch breakdown (3224508, it 38k): time to 1st / 2nd / 3rd attach q50 8.3 / 16.2 / 25.6 s (unchanged per catch since 30k; more episodes reach each); 2-attached spells end by timeout 37 / drop 5 / reached_all 3, spell length q50 19.2 s; at timeout the third drone is PARKED ~0.25 m from its bar (not closing over the last 2 s), hook ~0.07 m above the bar, 0 % over box, nearest holder 0.58 / 0.86 / 1.02 m; its episode-minimum hook-to-bar distance is 0.014 / 0.037 / 0.17 m, i.e. the median got inside the 5 cm radius without catching. Hypotheses (open): (a) legacy close penalty (separation_margin 0.8, collision_dist 0.5, close_penalty_end 20: ~-15 / s at 0.58 m from a holder, comparable to the +20 catch) deters the retry near holders on adjacent faces; (b) the near miss fails a catch condition other than radius (chord gate / upsweep). Near-miss split (3224513, it 38k) OVERTURNS the parked reading: dwells inside the 5 cm radius catch at 6.6 % (single) / 3.9 / 2.4 / 0 % (3-drone with 0 / 1 / 2 others attached; 0 of 92 at n = 2, p ~ 0.03); the dominant failed gate at every n is the 25 deg chord swing-out gate (71-83 %), upsweep 25 -> 47 % at n = 2; when all gates are met the surrogate draw still loses (p_max q50 0.28, 0.17 at n = 2); median miss distance 1.7-1.9 cm. Retry within 5 s after a miss: 0.89-0.93 at every n (not parked). Close penalty active in 30 % of n = 2 misses, essentially never at n = 0 / 1. Reading: the lever is the catch skill (swing-out at contact) and the surrogate catch probability, not spacing; holder interference is real but secondary. Diag 67c7f7d (near_miss_by_n_attached) deployed. Original text: (i) Add the safety floor term to reward_mode simple; it changes no success / catch / hold semantics and the scratch run is only ~2 h in, so restarting scratch with it costs little. (ii) If the fine-tune 3-drone numbers at 34k are still below 32k, stop the fine-tune arm and keep legacy (currently strongest: learning the vertical hold under the vbox ramp) + scratch (the clean test of the simple reward); use the freed GPU for a second legacy seed. PM recommendation: both (i) and (ii), decided on the 34k table. Floor term ready: flycrane-meta f701abb, `rs_k_floor` (default 0 = off) and `rs_floor_z` 0.45 m (= min_drone_height + 0.3); per drone -k dt max(0, floor_z - z). Suggested k = 40: a 0.3 m/s sink through the band costs -6, hovering 0.15 m below the floor -6 / s, vs catch +20, fly_low termination -40, legacy alive +15.6 / episode.
- **D8. Third-catch lever = single-drone catch skill at the final rule.** Per-dwell catch 4-7 %, the 25 deg swing-out gate fails in 71-83 % of dwells, and the surrogate draw loses 72 % of the time even when every gate is met (p_max q50 0.28). Options: (a) wait: the 3-drone curriculum reached the final 5 cm rule only around 36-38k and catches rose 76 -> 164 over 36k -> 38k, so the per-dwell rate may still be climbing; judge on f3_40000 / 42000 per-dwell catch and swing-out failure share; (b) potential-based shaping on the chord angle near the bar (the simple reward's Phi already has it; the legacy hook_swing term is per-step and was farmed); (c) reconsider the 25 deg gate against the real hook geometry (physics / surrogate decision); (d) revisit what sets p_max in the surrogate catch model. PM recommendation: (a) for two tracker points, with -6d reporting what sets p_max; then (b) or (c) only if the per-dwell rate is flat. UPDATE f3_40000 (point 1 of 2): per-dwell catch fell at every n (single 6.6 -> 4.5 %, n1 2.4 -> 1.2 %), the swing-out share of misses rose to 93 %, and the adaptive curriculum is found to OSCILLATE (progress 1.0 at ~904k, back to 0.55 / radius 0.086 m at 944k, climbing again), which explains the 55 / 121 / 76 / 164 / 112 tracker series. Reading: the policy cannot hold the final 5 cm rule, so more waiting is unlikely to help; the lever is on the gate side (relax the 25 deg swing-out gate, or raise the curriculum's radius floor so the rule stops cycling) rather than more shaping. Decide on f3_42000 (~3 h); -6d makes no change without the user. **ROOT CAUSE FOUND (-6d, 2026-10-20): the 40k drop is the drone-drone collision termination switching on, not the 5 cm radius.** The run's sbatch lists collision_curriculum_steps 400k / 650k / 900k and the last wins; collision termination applies only at _coll_frac >= 1 (construction.py:743, 803, 828), and the restored step counter (746k at it 31k, ~24 ctrl steps / it) crossed 900k at ~it 37.5k, the same window in which progress reached 1.0. Log: step 900k success 0.523 / 1448 drone-episodes -> step 902k success 0.262 / 2881 drone-episodes (episodes doubled = collisions ending them; the radius only moved 0.054 -> 0.050). Success drifted to 0.15, the adaptive rule backed off to 0.55, and it is now back at 0.85 with success ~0.5: the policy is relearning under collision termination. The 32k-36k oscillation predates this and is unexplained. **Protocol gap:** the 3-drone tracker runs diag_seq_hook.py with --no_collision (collision_dist 0, w_proximity 0; izar_v84freshtrack3.sbatch line 17), so every tracker protocol number so far is measured WITHOUT the collision termination that training (now) and the sequence env apply. The 40k safety change is real: pin force q50 3.13 -> 5.12 N, attached steps under the safe force 0.95 -> 0.26, lateral force at two attached 0.69 -> 2.22 N, risk > 1 dominated by slide (13051 / 13061 steps): drones pull harder and farther out, consistent with keeping separation. Geometry: collision_dist is 0.5 m in the run (cfg default 0.25), and adjacent wall centres are only sqrt(hx^2 + hy^2) = 0.45-0.51 m apart at half_xy 0.32-0.36, so the third drone's approach to its bar next to two holders is the constrained phase. **Curriculum rule** (construction.py:436-451): every adapt_every 2000 ctrl steps with >= 200 finished free-start drone-episodes, success > 0.5 -> progress +0.05, < 0.2 -> -0.05; progress drives capture radius 0.13 -> 0.05, swing-out angle 0 -> 25 deg, hold milestone 30 -> 100 steps and the v84 aids; there is no radius floor. Option (i) cap progress at 0.75 (new field adapt_max_progress, ~5 lines + regression_checks): radius 0.070, angle 18.75 deg, hold 82 steps; training success held 0.49 there before and after the collision onset. Option (ii) min_chord_angle_deg 25 -> 15 at radius 0.05: no geometric derivation for any value; 25 deg is the hook-grasp recipe value (hook comes onto the bar from outside). Both depart from the verified v84r2 rule (catch 0.91 / hold 0.98 at 25 deg / 5 cm, single drone); the single plant here is at protocol 0.27-0.38, so the gap is not only three-drone (differences: shared policy over two plants, wider box, 40 s, collision termination, virtual box). **p_max:** p = [0.02 + 0.88 clamp(v_up / 1.0)] x clamp((2.5 - |v_rel|) / 1.5) from the hook velocity at contact, no angle term; at 40k n0 the swing-out gate fails 1250 / 1342 misses (93 %) and the all-gates draw loses 81 (6 %), so (d) is at most a 6 % lever and (c) the gate is the lever. **PM recommendation (revised):** (1) D8 stays 'wait': judge 42k / 44k under collision termination (progress already back at 0.85); choose (i) or (ii) only if training success stays < 0.5 at progress 1.0. Oscillation analysis (-6d, training log, step ~ 746k + 24 (it - 31k)): 32k -> 34k (55 -> 121 catches) happened at constant progress 0.85 with training success wandering 0.23-0.46, so NOT curriculum-driven; 34k -> 36k (121 -> 76) coincides with one back-off 0.85 -> 0.75 at 836k-842k after a sub-0.2 window; 36k -> 38k (76 -> 164) with the climb 0.75 -> 1.0 at 882k-900k (success 0.49-0.54). The rule cycled once before the collision onset, and the success signal is noisy (0.2-0.46 between 2k-step windows at fixed progress) against the narrow 0.2 / 0.5 band, so one bad window steps it down. **This argues for hysteresis / smoothing of the adaptive rule (EMA over 3-5 windows or two consecutive windows) rather than a cap: a cap at 0.75 also stops the cycling but gives up the 5 cm / 25 deg final rule.** Added as option (v) to D8. Design of (v) (-6d, no code): cfg field `adapt_window: int = 1` (deque of the last N window success rates, step only on their mean; clear the deque after a step so a step needs N fresh windows; default 1 = current behaviour), ~10 lines in _curriculum_frac (construction.py:436), reward untouched, regression_checks before sync. Resume: trainer.py:114-115 / 153-156 save and restore env_ctrl_step and env_cur_frac, so a restart with the new rule continues from the saved progress (~0.85 now) and the collision ramp state; only the in-window accumulators and the deque start empty (a ~6k-step / ~250-it freeze at N = 3). **(v) is therefore free at the next restart of the running arm**, no reward change, no re-training cost; the resume must use the same family / cfg. Second instance: the scratch simple+floor arm shows the same over-shoot / back-off (progress 0.80 at ~105k steps, success 0.17, back to 0.65 by 150k; ssf_6000 dip with per-attempt skill unchanged), so the rule, not the reward, is cycling. Adapt-rule counts (2k-step windows): ssf scratch 0-162k: 81 windows, 16 up / 3 down, 72.8 % of windows inside the 0.2-0.5 dead band (net +13, progress 0.65); legacy s1 since the it-31k resume (746k-998k): 127 windows, 13 up / 11 down, 81.1 % in the band (net +2 over ~10k iterations = a near-random walk between 0.75 and 1.0). The rule moves on the noisy tails (19-27 % of windows); an N = 3 mean would have suppressed most single-window excursions (no exact counterfactual, since progress changes the success that follows). Tracker swings are policy variance + these training-distribution shifts (the tracker always evaluates at the final rule). (2) Decide on the collision radius instead: 0.5 m vs the 0.25 default and the 0.45-0.51 m wall spacing is a user choice (physical drone footprint vs conservatism) and the most likely cause of the outward-pull / slide regression; a smaller radius (0.3-0.35 m) is a config change at the next restart, not a reward change. Follow-up (-6d): wire_length 0.55 m (attachment.py:98), hold spawn = bar + 0.55 unit(0.8 n + 0.6 z) (0.44 m out, 0.33 m up), adjacent holders ~1.07-1.13 m apart, so the hold itself is clear of 0.5 m. The 0.5 m is a run override with no derivation in NOTES / status / git; parent-repo thresholds are 0.2 (hover_llc), 0.4 (track / obstacle), 0.6 (directMARL hover): a conservative inherited choice, not a measured footprint. The Falcon rotor-tip span has not been measured (readable from falcon.usd); Falcon footprint measured from falcon.usd (-6d, read-only, metersPerUnit 1): rotor centres at (+-0.075, +-0.075, 0.05) m (arm 0.106 m, matches indi.py l = 0.10607), prop radius ~0.055 m, body bbox 0.18 m square, prop-tip reach 0.16 m from the centre, tip-to-tip span ~0.32 m: two prop disks touch at a centre distance of ~0.32 m (worst case). **Suggested collision_dist 0.40 m** = 0.32 m contact + 0.08 m (25 %) margin for tracking error and tilt. Caveats: the metric is 3D centre distance, it ignores downwash (significant with one drone above another, not modelled in sim) and wire / hook entanglement; to cover downwash keep 0.5 m or add a vertical-offset term instead of a larger sphere; 0.25 m (cfg default) is below physical contact and should not be used. Side effect of --no_collision: the near-miss close_rate magnitudes used collision_dist 0 and are off; the gt0 / active fractions (0.8 m threshold) stand. Collision termination in eval: without the flag it applies from step 0 (collision_curriculum_steps defaults to 0 -> cfr 1). New diag `separation_by_n_attached` (closest-pair q10/50/90 and shares < 0.5 / 0.25 m per n, attached nearest-neighbour quantiles, episodes with min < 0.5 m, protocol episodes that went below 0.5 / 0.25 m before the hold completed); test job 3224525 on it_40000 with --no_collision (sepnc_) and with collision at 0.5 m (sepc_) = first WITH-collision protocol number; goes into the live tree before the 42k tracker call if clean. DONE: 3224525 clean, diag committed 769c06a (dev, pushed origin + alc, in the live izar tree). **it_40000 with collision at 0.5 m vs --no_collision (64 envs x 8000 steps): catches 103 vs 112, episodes 137 vs 128, protocol joint hold 0 vs 0, collision terminations 30 (22 % of episodes), k-attached share [0.56, 0.38, 0.062, 0.0002] vs [0.52, 0.40, 0.075, 0.0008].** No protocol episodes at 40k in either condition, so whether the rule cuts holds is open until 42k+. --no_collision closest pair q10/50/90: n0 0.60/0.78/1.04 m (1.5 % of steps < 0.5), n1 0.66/0.85/1.08 (1.8 %), n2 0.78/0.96/1.14 (0), n3 0.87/0.99/1.26 (0); no step below 0.25 m at any n; attached nearest neighbour 0.79/1.03/1.26 m; 14.8 % of episodes dip below 0.5 m (consistent with 22 % terminations). - **D18 (USER DESIGN STANCE, 2026-10-21 evening): no feedback between drones, ever: no orchestration or timing inputs, no observer, no inter-drone observation, and ideally no live payload pose either; each drone acts on its own proprioception + cable (INDI residual) and a BUILDING PLAN: each object's initial pose, the drone's relative wall attachment, and the object's target pose.** Implications recorded by the PM: (i) the current 51-dim actor frame already has no other-drone input; it does carry the LIVE box base + rotation (12 dims) + assigned wall centre (3), which equals the planned pose while the box is static (hooking) and becomes feedback to remove for transport, where it meets the known wall (drone-only obs cannot identify the payload orientation within tolerance), so the pre-registered transport question is now the central claim and D1a (pose-free 51x3 student from a pose-seeing teacher) stays consistent with it; (ii) collision avoidance must come from the PLAN'S GEOMETRY only: plan-based starts outside each drone's own wall along the wall normal, radial corridors that never cross the footprint; drone identities and sequencing permits rejected (identity not needed with per-episode wall assignment; a permit is a feedback signal); (iii) the cost: no recovery if a drone fails, transport orientation rests on what a drone infers from its own cable, which sharpens the Hebbian layer's job (attached flag frame-identifiable, box mass not). **Test (user go 'y', eval only, local GPU, -6d): R0 19k x3 zero-shot with plan-based radial starts, all released at t = 0, with / without collision 0.40, Z3 row format; all-attached under the rule rising from 0.145 toward 0.545 means the plan carries avoidance and training only adds the hold; no rise means the swing / hold collides and the sector penalty (unseen by R0) is next.** Running (-6d, ~22:00, ~/flycrane_tracker/r0_3drone/run_plan.sh, labels R0_19k_plan_nocoll / _coll040). - **D21 (APPROVED by the user 2026-10-27 ~17:15, AskUserQuestion in -6d, 'Approve v1.3 (Recommended)', reported to the PM by -6d; roll-out plan PM-reviewed below; PM proposal 2026-10-26 ~09:15): evaluation protocol v1.3 = measure the joint-hold row with joint_hold_steps 0 in the eval cfg (no joint-hold termination). Found by -6d while answering the PM's off-by-one check: there is no counting bug, but the eval cfg (joint_hold_steps 100, joint_hold_light false) ends an episode on its first 100 consecutive all-attached steps regardless of taut / risk, so the protocol success is a one-shot test that must be clean from its first step, which the section-3 definition does not require. All protocol numbers to date are lower bounds (0 everywhere; d16f 60k: 5.3 % of episodes reached the full all-attached second, none clean). Proposal text is in evaluation_protocol.md; v1.2 rows stay valid as lower bounds; the tracker carries both rows for one ladder. - **D24 (CANDIDATE, raised by the user 2026-10-29 ~14:55: 'should we give the INDI estimated acceleration norm as an input, so the drone knows how much it pulls?'; PM code reading ~15:00, not proposed as a run). FACTS: the 51-dim frame already ends with the 6-dim INDI disturbance residual (construction.py:537; flycrane_hook/controllers/indi.py:115): angular = alpha_cmd - omega_dot, linear = R^T (acc_cmd - a_meas) in the body frame, where acc_cmd = des_acc - g - acc_load and acc_load = a_meas - g - R T/m (geometric.py:162-169) is the controller's own estimate of the external specific force. So the linear residual = (des_acc - a_meas) + R T/m - a_meas: the wire pull is in it, but entangled with the tracking error and a ~9.81 m/s^2 thrust offset; the clean quantity the user asks for is acc_load itself (|acc_load| x 0.6017 kg = wire tension, world frame), computed every control step and NOT in the frame. The 2026-10-19 insight probes agree: box mass is decodable only through the residual and only weakly (R^2 0.2, insight_tasks §2.2), so the residual is a poor force sensor as encoded. PM VIEW: (i) a scalar norm throws away the direction, and direction is the whole story (horizontal pull = sliding, vertical = load sharing); prefer the 3-vector acc_load in the body frame; (ii) what the drone senses is the WIRE TENSION, not its force on the box: F_up_i = T_z - m_hook g, so 'how much it pulls on the box' needs the hook mass, which a clean tension signal gives for free while the hook hangs (T = m_hook g) -> this is the natural companion to hook-mass DR (D17); (iii) within D18 (own sensor, no inter-drone input); (iv) it is an observation-contract change (51 -> 54, or swap the residual's linear part for acc_load at 51), so it is for the run after d16t at the earliest, retrained from scratch, and the transport family / Hebbian layer must take the same frame; (v) it would not have rescued d16r: d16r parks because parking pays, not because it cannot sense its pull; d16t tests the reward side first. OPEN before proposing: a one-hour offline probe (-6d, insight tooling) of how well own wire tension / T_z / F_up_i decode linearly from the present 153-dim obs at d16r 48k; if R^2 is high the input is redundant, if low (as for box mass) the swap is cheap and justified. PROBE STAGED (-6d, 2026-10-29 ~15:10; PM-checked design): ~/flycrane_tracker/probe/{collect_pull.py, probe_pull.py, run_after_d16t4k.sh}, starts itself after the d16t 4k rows, pauses the tracker meanwhile; d16r 48k, 64 x 3000 under the d16rj contract, attached drone-steps, inputs obs153 / residual18 (dims 45:51 per frame) / acc_load3 / obs153 + acc_load3, targets |T| / T_z / F_up (the last two equal up to m_h g at the fixed 0.30 kg hook, so one number), linear + MLP-128, 20 % of episodes held out; result probe/pull_d16r48k_r2.json. Caveat: 'acc_load + hook mass' is only testable with varied hook masses (D17 setup). PROBE RESULT (2026-10-29 ~18:44, §7): own pull decodes from the present frame at R^2 0.65 (|T_wire|) / 0.73 (F_up) linear; acc_load appended lifts it to 0.84 / 0.92 linear, so the input is not redundant but the case is moderate; acc_load's scale is off (corr 0.805 with |T_wire|, R^2 -9.8 as newtons): the vertical gain is right but a ~10 N offset at a steady hold is unexplained (not the drone mass: asset 0.617 kg vs controller 0.6017); sim audit requested from -6d (§7) before the input is adopted. PM RECOMMENDS (a): run after d16t = hook-mass DR + acc_load appended (51 -> 54, residual kept) as one package, a second arm without the input if a slot is free; (b) acc_load only; (c) leave the frame. The frame change propagates to transport / evo (-0f) and the Hebbian layer.**

## 5. Risks

- A stalls below 10 % joint holds: per-drone catch is 1/3 per 20 s episode from free starts; three simultaneous
  holds compound. Mitigations in order: longer episodes (done: 40 s), partial-hooked-start curriculum kept at
  evaluation off, a hold bonus while others catch, and physics (softer pin, done).
- Training stability at curriculum hand-offs (2026-10-30 19:56, D26): d16u went NaN 440 iterations after curriculum end + vbox ramp begin (300k control steps) on the corrected drone; a job that dies this way shows as COMPLETED in sacct because the sbatch masks the exit code, and it went unnoticed 13 h. Mitigations: non-finite guard in the trainer, exit-code propagation in every sbatch, a squeue / sacct + train.log-mtime watcher on the laptop.
- Plant fidelity (2026-10-29, D25): the drone asset carries a phantom 1.0 kg link, so every hooking checkpoint was trained at thrust-to-weight 1.33 and a hook / drone ratio of 0.19; results before the fix do not transfer to the 0.6 kg Falcon, and the recipe may change once the drone can climb.
- B is unowned; every week it stays unowned the merge slips a week.
- The ES track consumes a GPU for 45 h for a development-plant number. Acceptable once; not again.
- Compute: one laptop, ALC, two izar slots. Steps 2 and 3 can run in parallel on ALC + izar.

## 6. Oversight cadence

- -6d reports at it 32k and 34k (decomposition, protocol joint hold, catch rate under the softer pin).
- -0f reports when v17's deterministic eval exists and sends the gap estimate for B.
- -c5 keeps this file, the protocol and the memory current; pushes peer commits after review (d4b27a1 pushed).

  Reading: P0 reaches the capture radius as often as the reference (0.58-0.72 vs ~0.7) but its swung-out share is 30-300x lower: it approaches without swinging out, the same miss as f3. P3 (above_box 60, w_tension 0.5, vbox) is the only arm of ours with a rising swOut share. ref 2k / 4k still queued. PM trainer diff vs rsl_rl 2.3.3 (code read, P0 flags): (i) std clamp 1.5 (rsl_rl: none) and P0 sits on it; (ii) the critic is a separate Adam at a fixed 1e-3 with an unclipped MSE (rsl_rl: one optimiser under the adaptive-KL lr, which floors at 1e-5-5e-5 on this task, clipped value loss, coef 1.0) so our critic moves 20-100x faster than v84's; (iii) minibatches are blocks of 16 tasks x all their 192 samples (rsl_rl: uniform shuffle), same size 3072; (iv) everything else matched (GAE + time-out bootstrap, global advantage normalisation, entropy on the summed log-std, grad clip 1.0, frozen-within-iteration obs normaliser vs rsl_rl's per-step empirical normaliser). **PM recommendation for the next arm (user's go needed in -6d): the definitive bisect is rsl_rl's own OnPolicyRunner on the construction single plant with P0's env_cfg, via the hook release's scripts/rsl_rl_env.py adapter (DirectMARLEnv -> VecEnv; ~1-2 h glue, ~4 h to 10k it). If rsl_rl learns the swing-out on our plant the trainer is proven and can be bisected or replaced for the hook phase; a cheap parallel arm P0b = P0 with max_std 3.0 tests (i) alone.** Nothing launched.
- **P0 traces (-6d, from P0's history CSV, no wandb on izar) and a P0 env deviation (2026-10-20 night).** (a) The std climbs because the entropy bonus (-0.004 per log-std element) outweighs the policy gradient on log-std, which does push the noise down but at about half the strength (g_logstd -0.0013..-0.0026 over 1k-9k, 21-46 % of minibatches positive, gn_std ~0.033): Adam turns the consistent net sign into a drift onto the 1.5 cap. PM reading: the entropy / policy-gradient balance is parametrisation-invariant under Adam (rsl_rl's scalar std vs our log-std give the same ratio), and v84r2's std stopped at 1.33 because it was catching (training attached_frac 0.03-0.08) and the advantage signal pushed back; **the pinned std is a symptom of no catch signal, not the cause**, so P0b (max_std 3.0) is dropped. (b) Critic loss 4.95 -> 0.5-0.8 by 8-9k, no blow-up. (c) lr only (rsl_rl 2.3.3 never logs KL, so no KL comparison exists): medians by bin v84r2 vs P0: 0-2k 3.4e-5 vs 1.5e-5; 2-5k 5.1e-5 vs 1.1-1.7e-4; 5-10k 2.3e-5 vs 0.8-1.7e-4; 10-20k v84r2 at the 1e-5 floor (its KL was mostly above 2x desired there). PM reading: under the same KL rule the per-update policy change is the invariant, so the lr level is not read as causal. Noise std by the same bins, v84r2 0.94 / 1.13 / 1.40 / 1.33 / 0.83 vs P0 0.99 / 1.13 / 1.35-1.49 / 1.497 pinned: through 2-5k v84r2 ran about as high as P0 and turned down from 5k when catches appeared, which supports the symptom reading. Value loss v84r2 3.5 / 8.4 / 3.6 / 4.7 / 8.3 vs P0 4.95 / 3.07 / 2.0-2.4 / 0.5-2.2: P0's critic loss falls because there are no catch rewards to predict. Init matches (nn.Linear default, head_norm off). (d) **P0's held starts differ from v84's**, so P0 is not a trainer-only arm: v84 (replication env.yaml) spawns held drones wire-length up-and-out along 0.8 n + 0.6 z with the pin yanking the hook onto the bar, position jitter 0 -> 0.15 m with curriculum progress, 0.4 m/s up-and-out velocity, facing the wall; P0 hangs the hook exactly on the bar (hold_spawn_v84=false), jitter 0, velocity 0, yaw jitter 3.14 also on held starts. All four act only during the first ~2.7k it (hold_start 0.5 -> 0 over 65k steps, which v84 also has) but shape the early hold / swing prior. Fix = hold_spawn_v84=true hold_spawn_v0=0.4 + a small jitter-ramp code addition. Swing assist already matched. (e) rsl_rl-on-construction feasibility: ~0.5-1 day + smoke; the family layer adds the hold-start curriculum (move it env-side as a decaying frac, as v84 does), the actor frame stack, a constant per-drone one-hot in the critic state, stagger (= rsl_rl init_at_random_ep_len) and a reward outlier guard; the hook release's rsl_rl_env.py (117 lines) is the base. **Revised PM recommendation (user's go in -6d): two arms, one change each: (1) P0c = P0 + v84 held starts (env-side, cheap, ~4 h to 10k) tests the early prior; (2) rsl_rl OnPolicyRunner on the construction single plant with P0c's env tests the trainer. Read at 6k / 8k / 10k against the reference rows above.** USER: "launch both" (in -6d): P0c 3224809 and R0 3224820 running since ~2026-10-20 evening; PM review of 85fee82 / 348df14 (actor frame stack is env-side via actor_history_len so the adapter sees 153; jitter ramp keyed to v84's 65k attached-spawn curriculum; rsl_rl 2.3.3 interface: time_outs bootstrap, critic key, episode_length_buf setter all matched): no defect found; meta dev 348df14 pushed to origin + alc. Reading rule for the pair: R0 catching on v84's schedule (~39 / 150 at 6k) and P0c not = trainer; both catching = the held-start prior; neither = an env difference the ladder has not isolated (next suspects: the frame-stack / normaliser path, reward scale). **P0 10k row (landed): 6 / 138 catches, within-capture 0.53, swOut 3.6e-4, all gates 1.0e-4 = the reference's 4k row (9 / 132, 0.53, 5.5e-4): P0 is LATE by ~5-6k iterations, not broken** (first swung-out signal; swOut was 0-9e-5 at 6-8k).   **D16 COLLAPSED at 1k (2026-10-21 ~10:30): catches 192 vs R0's 760 without the rule, all-attached 0.02 vs 0.545; under the rule 0.0058 vs 0.145 with the same collisions per episode (0.78 vs 0.75), 114 of 126 terminations fly_low (median drone z 0.16 m, nothing attached); train all_attached_frac 0.13 -> 0.01-0.04 over it 0-1.6k. Read: the fresh critic at adaptive lr 1e-3 plus hard termination from step 0 destroyed the catching skill in under 1k iterations without buying avoidance. D16b (user: 'do the proposed things' + 'make collisions soft reward punished first before hard termination'): critic warm-up 300 updates with the actor frozen, adaptive lr capped at 1e-4, collisions soft for 2k it (per-drone close penalty 1 -> 10 inside 0.6 m, no termination) then hard at 0.40 m; code flycrane-meta d644b49 (--critic_warmup, --max_lr; PM-reviewed, no defect; smoke_b actor drift 0.0 at it 0, 0.0007 at it 5); user plan: leave D16 to its 2k read, cancel if no recovery (user's word for scancel), launch D16b.** **D16b COLLAPSED THE SAME WAY, EARLIER (rows 1k-3k, 2026-10-21 ~13:45): 124 catches at 1k after only 700 unfrozen updates at lr <= 1e-4, 15 at 2k; fly_low 116 -> 162 of ~190 episodes; train attached_frac 0.83 (it 400, actor still frozen) -> 0.34-0.56 by 4k, std 0.79 -> 1.16, episode length 20 s -> 5-13 s. The warm-up and the lr cap did not stop the skill loss, so the optimiser is not the main cause. Hypothesis (-6d): the three-drone REWARD makes catching / holding net-negative for R0's behaviour, so the policy drifts toward not attaching and the drones sink. PM addition: the one structural difference from R0's training is end_on_hold_success = false with 40 s episodes, i.e. R0 was trained to catch, hold 100 steps and END with the bonus, while here it must keep holding under per-step costs (action, force, downwash, vbox risk, pin) for the rest of the episode; if the per-term rates show the attached phase net-negative, that is the fix target. Test running locally (~15:00, results ~45 min): tests/reward_rates.py --family seq_hook (uncommitted) banks each drone's per-step reward terms by phase (free / attached / all_attached), team terms counted fully per drone, blow-up envs excluded; three runs at 64 envs x 3000 steps, deterministic, final gates: R0_P0c = R0 19k in its own seq_hook1 env (free starts, curricula at final), R0_D16b = R0 19k in D16b's post-2k env (close penalty 10, hard 0.40, hold starts 0.15 + partial 0.3), D16b3k_D16b = D16b it 3000 in that env. Second structural difference found by -6d: R0 trained with vbox_check = false, while D16 / D16b have vbox_check = true with vbox_penalty_start 1.0 LIVE FROM STEP 0 (only the ramp to the full 10 starts at 300k) plus w_vbox_pull 1.0 toward 0.7; so the vbox and vbox_pull terms tax the attached phase from the first iteration, correcting the PM note above that the vbox ramp was off. D16b keeps running until the rates read; -6d brings the user a recommendation.** **First (non-phase) rates (-6d, ~15:30, D16b's post-2k env, deterministic, per env-second): R0 19k total 44.3 (308 catches / 267 eps; hold_success 11.3, hold_progress 9.0, joint_hold_success 3.96, catch 3.2, sector -0.86, vbox -0.21, close -1.11, terminate -9.0) vs D16b it 3000 total 14.1 (12 catches; sector -4.5, terminate -9.1). The D16b reward pays R0's behaviour 3x more than what D16b learned, so 'catching is net-negative' is refuted; vbox terms are small. -6d's next hypothesis: exploration noise (training std 0.8 -> 1.16, eval deterministic), stochastic reward_rates runs queued.** **PM read of D16b's own training log (rsl_rl stdout, /work/lis/vandigge/results_d16b/d16b_logs/train.log, means per iteration window; control 100 Hz, 40 s = 4000 steps):**

  reward_outliers 0 throughout (guard cleared); dones are env-level and identical on the three rows (construction.py _get_dones returns the same term / tout for every agent), so the row bookkeeping is not corrupting GAE. **Reading: the STOCHASTIC frozen R0 (it 20-400, std 1.0, the policy PPO started from) earned 0.22-0.25 per row-step and ~400 per episode on the training reward; after unfreezing, with the std nearly unchanged (0.8-1.2), PPO walked the policy DOWN to 0.08 per row-step by 1.5k, i.e. it descended its own logged objective by 3x. That refutes the noise hypothesis in advance (noise did not make R0's behaviour poor: the frozen stochastic rate matches the deterministic rate order of magnitude and is the best point of the whole run) and leaves two explanations: (1) the gamma = 0.99 / lam = 0.95 objective (horizon ~100 steps = 1 s at 100 Hz) is myopic relative to the catch: the swing phase pays sector / close / action costs for seconds before the hold stream, so the discounted advantage of starting a catch is negative even though the rate is positive, which R0 escaped in the single-drone env (no sector -0.9, no close -1.1, no crowding, held starts 50 %); (2) a learning-signal defect in the three-drone path (per-row values / returns / critic credit) that the reward and dones checks do not cover. Discriminators proposed to -6d: (a) the gamma-discounted return per row-step on the stochastic rollouts (frozen R0 vs D16b 3k): if it also fell, (2); if it rose while the rate fell, (1); (b) a SINGLE-DRONE control through the same adapter (--family seq_hook1, R0 init, warm-up + cap, P0c's env but end_on_hold_success = false and 40 s episodes, ~1k it): decay there means the episode / objective structure, no decay means the three-drone path. Also note the vbox pull is live from step 0 and the time-out share rises to full 40 s episodes at it 600-2000 (hang-back survives) before hard 0.40 shortens them again.** **Both discriminators set up (-6d, ~16:00): (a) reward_rates.py now reports the gamma-0.99 discounted return per row-step (cut at episode ends, last 500 steps dropped) and the mean reward per row-step; the remaining phase runs (R0_D16b, D16b3k_D16b) and the three stochastic runs carry it. (b) single-drone control izar_d16ctl.sbatch 3226326 DONE (both EXIT 0, 1k it each; read ~23:00): **C1 DECAYS: the fine-tune recipe breaks R0 even single-drone in its own env.** Rew per step (attached_frac): C1 frozen it 100-300 0.21 (0.10), it 400 after the first 100 unfrozen updates 0.12 (0.03), it 500-900 0.087-0.098 (0.01-0.04), std 0.98; C2 (+ no end-on-hold, 40 s) 0.13-0.14 (0.75-0.81) -> 0.14 (0.75) -> 0.15 (0.37-0.58), std 1.0 -> 0.74 (reward per step held up by the hold stream while catching degrades too). So: not the three-drone path (probe), not mainly the hold structure; the collapse within ~100 updates at lr <= 1e-4 is systematic and the common factor is the INIT RECIPE: actor-only init with a fresh critic and fresh Adam state vs R0's own continued training. PM hypothesis for the mechanism: fresh Adam moments give every parameter a full lr-sized step in the sign of its current noisy gradient (bias-corrected first steps), ~2000 Adam steps per 100 updates, which perturbs a sharp optimum (catching needs precision) in all 256x256 weights at once; R0's own Adam had large second moments on the sensitive parameters and stepped them gently; a fresh critic adds noisy advantages but cannot by itself give a consistent direction. Note D16b's 300-update critic warm-up was not enough either, so 'fresh critic' means a critic far below R0's 19k-iteration fit, not merely untrained. **D16d implication: R0's critic must carry over to three drones.** Its input is 229 (state 228 + one-drone tag) vs 447 (three per-drone blocks in fixed order + one-hot). **D16d BUILT, PROBED and PM-REVIEWED (2026-10-21 ~20:15, flycrane-meta 3628049, scripts/train_rsl_rl.py only, md5 bb1ce8b8; staged as fm_d16d = fm_d16b + that file and izar_d16d.sbatch = D16b's minus --critic_warmup / --max_lr plus --init_critic --ego_critic --critic_clip 5; --max_lr 1.2e-4 added; SUBMITTED on the user's go in -6d, 2026-10-21 ~20:45, as izar 3226526, pending; tracker rows d16d_3drone / d16d_3dronec every 1k once the user restarts the local tracker).** Design: --ego_critic orders each row's critic input in its own drone's view: the one-drone layout first (per frame: common 24, own per-drone block 30 = pos, rot, vel, angvel, hook-drone, hook_vel, attached, tension, to_bar, dist; goal error 12; own phase 5; mass; attached share; own hold progress; joint timer; vbox = 76, R0's frame; x 3 history = 228 = R0's input), then the other drones' per / phase / hold blocks (36 each) in order k+1, k+2 (444 total; the drone one-hot is dropped: ego order replaces it and R0's one-hot column normalised to 0). lift_critic copies R0's first-layer weights onto the own-view columns, zero weights on the other drones' columns, normaliser statistics copied per field from the own block, and the 75 columns constant in R0's env (std < 1e-3: vbox, lift / manipulate phases, joint timer) get zero weight and 0 / 1 stats so they cannot explode and remain trainable. Probe (~/flycrane_tracker/d16/probe_lift.py, D16b env, R0 deterministic, 400 steps x 96 rows): layout exact; max |V3 - V_R0(own view)| 4.9e-4 at |V| ~ 160; after one PPO iteration 94 % of the other-drone weights non-zero. New finding: the exact lift gives V = -803 on ATTACHED rows (free +7.3) because three-drone attached states hit R0's frozen normaliser at up to 57 sigma (drone angvel 57, release-phase flag 39, tension 30, hold progress 22, joint timer 18: the hold never ends without end_on_hold_success); --critic_clip 5 clamps the normalised critic input to +-5: attached +45, free unchanged, so the lift is exact on free rows and the critic relearns attached values. - **DR CURRICULUM CONSTRAINT + SECTOR REDESIGN DRAFT (-6d, 2026-10-25 ~03:30). Box size cannot be scheduled within a run: box_size_dr scales each env's block prim once in _setup_scene before physics starts (replicate_physics False); rescaling a rigid prim mid-sim is unsupported by the tensor API. Options: (a) box_size_dr_frac, a fixed share of envs random-sized, the rest nominal (setup-time only); (b) two-stage run, nominal box until the catch curriculum ends then a full resume (actor + critic + normaliser) with DR on (needs a trainer resume flag; --init_checkpoint restores the actor only); (c) train with DR on and accept the onset delay (g_dr: costs the onset, not the swing). The bar band CAN be scheduled (_bar_lo per-env tensor; a ramp means recomputing _bar_lo / _bar_hi / _zm / _fc / _ref_bf); g_dr dropped DR and band together, so which carries the onset cost is unknown. DECISION (PM, agreeing with -6d): (c) for the next DR-on three-drone run, since d16f learns on R0 geometry and its walls are vbox and collisions, not the onset; (a) is the fallback if a DR-on run stalls, (b) is not worth a trainer flag. SECTOR DRAFT (uncommitted, local flycrane-meta dev on top of fm_d16c's env code, byte-identical otherwise; PM pre-review of the diff 14:10, no defect): cfg sector_ramp_begin / sector_ramp_end (control steps, 0 -> w_sector, off when end <= begin, same shape as vbox_ramp) and sector_attached_only (gate = 1 - na where na = unattached flag, so ATTACHED drones pay, free drones explore unpenalised); env: w_s = w_sector x ramp fraction, term x gate; defaults reproduce the old formula exactly. Two review notes: (i) the ramp is keyed on _ctrl_step, so 'after the catch curriculum' holds only with curriculum_steps (not adaptive_curriculum, which runs on _cur_frac); (ii) with sector_attached_only the inward term must stay disarmed (sector_inward_margin 10 as in d16f), or the hold pose over the wall is taxed. TESTED AND COMMITTED LOCALLY by -6d as flycrane-meta dev 2633497 (under the user's blanket command permission; +10 / -1 lines in construction.py / construction_cfg.py, matches the pre-reviewed diff; NOT pushed, NOT synced to izar, regression_checks before any sync): R0 it_05000 on R0's eval plant + w_sector 30 + inward margin 10, single, 128 x 3000: default sector -39.247 (identical to before the change); sector_attached_only 0.0; ramp 300k -> 900k at step 0 0.0; catches 1 and positive return 130.0 in all three. - **PLANT DEFECT FOUND: THE SIMULATED DRONE WEIGHS 1.62 kg, NOT 0.62 kg; A PHANTOM 1.0 kg LINK FROM THE URDF IMPORT (-6d sim audit 2026-10-29 18:52-18:53, ~/flycrane_tracker/probe/audit_accload.py, audit.log; PM URDF check 18:54; root log 18:55). root_physx_view.get_masses() per body: Falcon_base_link 1.0, Falcon_base_link_inertia 0.6, rotors 4 x 0.0042, wire 8 x 0.0005 + 8 x 0.0001, hook 0.30; total 1.9216 kg. Cause (PM-confirmed in the asset): flyhook.urdf's 'Falcon/base_link' has NO <inertial> element (base_joint fixed to base_link_inertia, which carries the 0.6 kg); the Isaac URDF importer gives such a link 1.0 kg although the converter config says link_density 0.0 (data/flyhook/config.yaml, converted 2026-07-07). The audit clears both PM suspects: body_acc_w equals the finite-difference acceleration (0.01-0.6 m/s^2), ~0 at hover, no -g in it; produced thrust equals the carried weight. Numbers: free hover with the hook hanging T 18.83 N = 1.9216 x 9.81, acc_load (0, 0.4, -21.5) = 9.81 - 18.83 / 0.6017; parked hold T 15.9-16.5 N = 1.62 kg x g, pin 2.95 N = hook weight, acc_load_z -16.6 to -17.6. So acc_load = -W/m_c - (m_t/m_c - 1)(a + g) with m_t/m_c = 2.69: a constant -16.3 m/s^2 at rest that rotates with tilt and scales with own acceleration, exactly the offline fit's residual. CONSEQUENCES: (1) every run on this asset (flycrane-meta construction / sequence / hook, flycrane-hook-grasp incl. the v84 line and the public release; the evo stream's flycrane_rod asset is UNCHECKED, -0f asked) flew a 1.62 kg drone with a controller modelled for 0.6017 kg; INDI treats the extra 1 kg as a permanent load disturbance and the INDI residual in every policy's frame has carried a gravity-sized constant (absorbed by the normaliser at level, but tilt-dependent: a spurious attitude cue a real drone's residual will not have). (2) THRUST HEADROOM: thrust_max 6.25 N per rotor, 25 N collective (indi.py:47-49); hover at 18.8 N leaves a thrust-to-weight of 1.33 against ~2.8 for the real Falcon with the same hook (25 / 9.0 N): the sim drone can barely climb or pump the swing, a plausible root of the single-drone catch difficulty (the 'swing-out wall', D16 series) and of the drones' preference for resting the hook. (3) The D17 sweep is a sweep of the hook / drone mass ratio: trained 0.30 / 1.62 = 0.19; the real Falcon with the same hook is 0.48, nearest to the 0.45 kg cell (0.28) but with different drone dynamics, so the sweep understates the sim-to-real gap. (4) D24: a clean pull input needs the mass fixed first; after the fix acc_load = -W/m_c up to the 2.5 % inertia-link mismatch. FIX OPTIONS (staged, not applied; user's call because every checkpoint and the running d16t / d16r are on the heavy drone): (a) env-side, 2 lines: add Falcon_base_link to the set_body_masses calls at construction.py:62 with ~1e-3 kg (no asset re-conversion, per project, visible in cfg); (b) asset-side: an <inertial> of ~0 on Falcon/base_link (or merge_fixed_joints) + USD re-conversion, which fixes hook-grasp and the release too. -6d asked to stage (a), run the four regression checks under it (pin / vbox numbers will move with the drone dynamics), and run ONE eval cell: d16r 48k on the corrected drone (sens framework), to see what of the trained policy survives the right mass. USER DECISION D25 (§4): fix when (now: relaunch d16t as d16u on the corrected drone, or after d16t), and whether 0.6017 kg is the real Falcon.**
- **D24 acc_load SCALE: NOT A FIXED GAIN; THE CONTROLLER'S EXTERNAL-FORCE ESTIMATE CARRIES AN UNEXPLAINED ~10 N OFFSET AT A STEADY HOLD, AND -6d's '1.6 kg DRONE' INFERENCE IS CONTRADICTED BY THE ASSET (-6d CPU fits on pull_d16r48k.pt 2026-10-29 ~18:45; PM asset check 18:50). -6d's fits (W = wire pull on the hook, world frame): |W| ~ |acc_load| a 0.434 b -4.79 N R^2 0.65; W_z ~ acc_z a -0.557 b -9.06 N R^2 0.77 (slope within 7 % of -m_c = -0.6017, so the vertical GAIN is right); W_x / W_y ~ acc_x / acc_y R^2 0.28 / 0.32 (horizontal components carry little); means acc_load (-1.1, -1.2, -20.1) m/s^2, W (0.63, 0.75, 2.12) N; residual after the fit (std 1.35 N) correlates with cos tilt (-0.36) and attitude / rate entries, not with acc_z. -6d's reading: acc_load = (a + g)(1 - m_t/m_c) - W/m_c with a simulated drone mass m_t ~ 1.6 kg. PM CHECK: the asset says otherwise: flyhook.urdf base 0.600 + 4 rotors 0.0042 = 0.617 kg (wire 8 x 0.5 g + dummies, hook 0.30 kg set by cfg), so m_t / m_c = 1.025 and the mass-mismatch term is ~0.25 m/s^2, not 16; with a = 0 and T = drone weight + W_z the formula predicts acc_load_z ~ -(6.05 + W_z) / 0.6017 + 9.81 ~ -3.8 for the mean W_z, against -20 measured: ~10 N of thrust, or 16 m/s^2 of acceleration, unaccounted for. Candidates (PM): (i) body_acc_w comes straight from PhysX get_link_accelerations (IsaacLab articulation_data.py:406), whose gravity convention and reliability for articulation links are not established here (later Isaac Lab versions finite-difference it); if lin_acc includes -g the '-g' in the formula does not cancel and the whole T/m_c appears (predicted intercept -10, fit -16, closer but not closed); (ii) the thrust the controller sees (self._forces from the motor model) vs the force the sim applies at the rotor bodies (construction.py:250-254, body frame). Not resolvable by reading; a one-env audit in sim is requested from -6d (print T, body_acc_w, finite-difference acceleration from body_com_vel_w, W, acc_load, get_masses() at hover and at a parked hold). CONSEQUENCE FOR D24: the probe's R^2 numbers stand (they include this bias), the input stays a moderate case, but acc_load should not be added to the frame until the offset is understood, because a state-dependent bias through the INDI residual also touches every run's controller. -6d confirmed: the hook weight is not counted twice; current_collective_thrust is the PRODUCED thrust of the previous substep (one-substep lag, not command vs produced).**

TARGET CORRECTION: _hook_force is the PIN force on the hook, not the wire tension; hook statics give W = -P + m_h g z, so targets are |T_wire| (q10/50/90 2.75 / 3.08 / 8.21 N, std 2.58), T_z = F_up + m_h g (same R^2 as F_up at the fixed hook), F_up (-2.96 / -2.20 / +3.78 N, std 2.80), |pin| (3.19 / 3.91 / 6.02). R^2 on held-out episodes [|T_wire| / F_up / |pin|], linear | MLP-128: obs153 0.65 / 0.73 / 0.44 | 0.61 / 0.80 / -0.06; residual18 0.48 / 0.53 / 0.26 | 0.68 / 0.74 / 0.47; acc_load3 0.67 / 0.80 / 0.30 | 0.81 / 0.86 / 0.64; obs153 + acc_load3 0.84 / 0.92 / 0.51 | 0.73 / 0.91 / 0.27. Sanity: 0.6017 x |acc_load| correlates 0.805 with |T_wire| but has R^2 -9.8, so the estimate is NOT the tension in newtons (a gravity / thrust-model offset; to be understood before the input is used, a normaliser absorbs scale but not a state-dependent bias). READ (PM, agreeing with -6d): the pull is far more visible than box mass (F_up 0.73 linear vs 0.2) but below the 0.8 'redundant' bar for |T_wire| (0.65); acc_load alone matches the whole frame and beats it non-linearly; appended to the frame it lifts the LINEAR read-out of |T_wire| 0.65 -> 0.84 and of F_up 0.73 -> 0.92. A moderate case for D24, not a strong one: the input is not redundant, it is what makes the pull linearly available to a small head. Caveats: 68 episodes, MLP rows over-fit (|pin| MLP negative); the '+ hook mass' row is uninformative at a fixed hook mass (D17 setup needed). PM RECOMMENDATION (§4 D24): include acc_load (3-vector, body frame, appended, 51 -> 54; keep the residual, which carried the hold in the hook-grasp task) in the run after d16t TOGETHER with hook-mass DR, since inferring the hook from the hanging tension is where linear availability matters; the transport / evo frame must follow (user decision, -0f to be told after it). Files: probe/pull_d16r48k.pt, pull_d16r48k_r2.json.**

READ (PM, agreeing with -6d): (i) catches halve between 0.15 and 0.20 kg (141 -> 164 vs 232), i.e. a hook 1/3 lighter than trained costs 30 % of the catches, 2/3 lighter costs 61 %; (ii) the all-three state collapses below the trained mass (845 steps at 0.20 kg vs 14k at 0.30; none at 0.15 / 0.10), so the hold does not transfer downward at all; (iii) the failure mode at 0.20 kg is NOT parking but over-pull: the team lift median turns positive (+0.24 m g, vs -0.58 at 0.30) because the pull learned for a 0.30 kg hook lifts a 0.20 kg hook off the box, and the all-three safe share drops to 0.55, i.e. the virtual box ends the hold by sliding; (iv) heavier hooks park more (-0.85 m g at 0.45) and hold more firmly (0.97). So d16r's hold is literally the hook's weight resting on the box: lighter hook -> no hold, heavier hook -> a heavier parked hold. ANSWER TO THE USER'S QUESTION ('how much are we overfitting on our setup; the hook might be lighter'): strongly, on the hook mass specifically; a 0.15-0.20 kg real hook would lose a third to half of the catches and all of the hold with this policy. d16t's gate (force on the box >= 0.05 m g per drone) is defined without the hook mass, so a d16t policy that passes it has lifted the box, not rested on it, but nothing in d16t makes the swing-out robust to a lighter hook either. Tracker restored (-6d): d16t / d16tj rows from it_04000 added to local_tracker.py (backup .bak_d16t), restart after the d16r_3dronec 56k row; resumed 14:50; mu / pin_kp cells deferred; sweep script + checkpoint kept in ~/flycrane_tracker/sens.**

The catch skill was tuned on this hook (v84 tip / hook weight; the swing-out is the exploration barrier) and the parked hold d16r converges on USES the hook weight exactly (a resting hook = -2.94 N on the box; the light gate |pin| <= 0.3 m g passes it in ~90 % of training envs and sits on a knife edge at the 1.0 kg eval box). So the honest answer is: strongly specialised; nothing in the recipe is shown to transfer across hook mass, friction or pin stiffness, and the one thing we know about hook mass is that the policy exploits it. D22 v2's gate on the force ON THE BOX (not |pin|) is hook-mass-invariant by definition, a point for it. PLAN (no user decision needed, laptop only): eval-only sensitivity sweep of d16r 48k under the eval contract with hook_mass in {0.10, 0.15, 0.20, 0.30, 0.45} kg, then vbox_mu {0.4, 0.8} and pin_kp {100, 250} at 0.30 kg (64 x 8000, seed 2026, jh0 / v1.3; catches, held, all-three, protocol_v13 light / parked / shared per cell; ~15 min each, after the d16t diff and behind the tracker). Readout: the hook mass at which catches or holds halve. DECISION FOR THE USER AFTER THE SWEEP: whether the next run after d16t adds hook-mass DR (a range instead of the scalar, ~3 lines) and a train / eval split on it (D17 as originally posed), and what the target hook mass of the real system is.**

- **REWARD-TERM INVENTORY FOR THE ALL-ATTACHED STATE (d16r cfg; -6d reading-only, 2026-10-29 ~04:55, ~25 min; PM verified the load-bearing lines on local HEAD b48f295 ~04:57): (a) training box mass U(0.9, 1.9) kg (construction_cfg.py:91), (b) 'tension' = |pin force| (construction.py:286), i.e. |m_h g - T| along a vertical wire, zero at T = hook weight 2.94 N, NOT at F_up = 0; a fully parked hook reads |pin| ~ 2.94 N. f_safe = 0.3 m g = 2.65-5.59 N (4.12 N at 1.4 kg), so a parked hook passes the light gate in ~90 % of training envs and the per-drone light band is T in [0, 7.1] N at 1.4 kg (team up to ~21 N vs m g 13.7 N). Terms per drone per s with all three hooks resting (F_up <= 0, wire straight): hold_lock +6; hold_tension +5 (geometric / kinematic); hold_progress +10; hold_success +80 once; hold_gentle -2.5 /N x (|pin| - f_safe)+ = 0 for m >= 1.0 kg (never charges T < hook weight); joint_hold_success +200 team once at 100 consecutive steps of all attached & |pin| <= f_safe (construction.py:707-710), parked qualifies; vbox penalty and vbox_pull MASKED to 0 once all attached (vbox_until_all, construction.py:752-762); w_force 0.5 x exp(-thrust / 6.25) favours parking by ~+0.03/s; w_tension 0, lift 0 (box fixed); termination off. NET: ~+21/s per drone (+63/s team) plus +200 terminal in the all-attached state, NO term depends on the sign or size of per-drone F_up or sum F_up, and the only force gate admits parked hooks; light, parked and lifting to ~1.5 m g are paid the same, and parked is the cheapest to reach and keep (lowest thrust, no slide / tip charge, vbox_pull rewards pulling toward risk 0.7 right up to the third catch). Dormant terms exist (w_hold_horizontal, w_hold_vertical, construction.py:763-772, both masked by not_all). IMPLICATIONS (PM): a shared-load term must gate on the per-drone vertical pull minus hook weight (the diag's F_up), not |pin|; to steer the +200 it must enter the joint-success condition, not sit beside it; the eval box is 1.0 kg where f_safe = hook weight exactly (knife edge). D22 draft staged in the PM scratchpad (d22_proposal.md), to be put to the user after the 48k call; not before.**
